fun {K} [Field.{0} K] [NumberField.{0} K] S W [WeierstrassCurve.IsElliptic.{0} W] hFull =>
  ZInQ.Manuscript.RC53Assist.CentralSelmer.scalarChangedComplex PadicInt.Coe.ringHom
    (ZInQ.Manuscript.RC53Assist.CentralKummer.compactKummerComplex S W hFull)
